Surah: 35. Follow the brief below (surah_map.md) exactly. The evidence is text.md (the surah), dictionary.md (every root of the surah's words, each branch with the classical dictionaries' own phrases), channels.md and hft.md (both are earlier readers' proposals: ignore their judgements (grades, strength or confidence labels, reading types, statements of what a reading may or may not do) and make your own) and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Return only the map as your final message.

===== _commentary/v16/prompts/r2/surah_map.md =====
Read the surah as one text and write its map of image chains: the lexical images that run through several of
its ayat and join them into one scene, process or movement. A later writer will read one ayah at a time, with
only that ayah's own dictionary. The map lets that writer hear what the ayah's words carry in the surah as a
whole, including senses whose evidence sits under the words of other ayat.

Your evidence is the surah text, the dictionary of every root in the surah (each branch with the classical
dictionaries' own phrases), earlier readers' channel review (channels.md) and activation hypotheses (hft.md),
and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Where a member or passage comes from memory rather than from
the dictionary or the text, say so. Do not use tools, delegate, browse or inspect files.

The channel review and the HFT records are proposals by earlier readers. Ignore their judgements: grades,
strength or confidence labels, reading types, words such as "surprising", "exploratory" or "latent", and every
statement of what a reading may or may not do. Make your own judgement from the surah's words, the dictionary
phrases and the Quran. Do not rediscover what they already assembled: start from their chains, test each one
against the dictionary phrases and the text, and join, extend, split or correct them. Where their wording
abstracts a member, go back to the dictionary phrase and name what it actually says.

A chain belongs on the map when its members are senses the dictionary attests for words that stand in the
surah, and together they make one image or process that the surah's wording or sequence lets a listener hear.
A member need not be the sense that translates its word in its own ayah; a chain is heard across the surah,
not in one word. Say plainly when a member is a rare sense or is attested only inside a fixed expression, and
keep it. When the dictionary itself joins two of the surah's words in one phrase, quote that phrase: it is
the strongest evidence a chain can have. Keep a scene at the level of its objects, their parts and their
operation. When proposals share members, do not fold one into another's more abstract function unless nothing
concrete is lost. Carry every chain that meets this test, however unusual; leave out proposals that do not.

Write the map in English, with Arabic quoted exactly (surah wording from the text, dictionary phrases from the
dictionary). It is working material for the writer, not commentary prose.

1. `## Chains`. For each chain, a `###` heading naming the image. One paragraph on what the image is and how it
   moves through the surah. Then its members, one line each: the ayah ref, the word as it stands in the text,
   the root and branch id, the dictionary's own phrase quoted exactly, and what this member contributes to the
   image. Add Quran passages outside the surah, with exact refs, where they stage or confirm the chain.
2. `## Ayat`. For each ayah in order: which chains it opens, advances or completes, and what its words add to
   each.
3. `## Not carried`. Each channel subchannel and each HFT record you did not carry into a chain, one line each:
   its name and why.

No ranking and no labels of strength or confidence. No list of what a writer must include. There is no length
target and no required number of chains or members.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s035/surah.r2/text.md =====
# Surah 35

- 35:1 ٱلْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ فَاطِرِ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ جَاعِلِ ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةِ رُسُلًا أُو۟لِىٓ أَجْنِحَةٍۢ مَّثْنَىٰ وَثُلَٰثَ وَرُبَٰعَ ۚ يَزِيدُ فِى ٱلْخَلْقِ مَا يَشَآءُ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَلَىٰ كُلِّ شَىْءٍۢ قَدِيرٌۭ
- 35:2 مَّا يَفْتَحِ ٱللَّهُ لِلنَّاسِ مِن رَّحْمَةٍۢ فَلَا مُمْسِكَ لَهَا ۖ وَمَا يُمْسِكْ فَلَا مُرْسِلَ لَهُۥ مِنۢ بَعْدِهِۦ ۚ وَهُوَ ٱلْعَزِيزُ ٱلْحَكِيمُ
- 35:3 يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلنَّاسُ ٱذْكُرُوا۟ نِعْمَتَ ٱللَّهِ عَلَيْكُمْ ۚ هَلْ مِنْ خَٰلِقٍ غَيْرُ ٱللَّهِ يَرْزُقُكُم مِّنَ ٱلسَّمَآءِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ ۚ لَآ إِلَٰهَ إِلَّا هُوَ ۖ فَأَنَّىٰ تُؤْفَكُونَ
- 35:4 وَإِن يُكَذِّبُوكَ فَقَدْ كُذِّبَتْ رُسُلٌۭ مِّن قَبْلِكَ ۚ وَإِلَى ٱللَّهِ تُرْجَعُ ٱلْأُمُورُ
- 35:5 يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلنَّاسُ إِنَّ وَعْدَ ٱللَّهِ حَقٌّۭ ۖ فَلَا تَغُرَّنَّكُمُ ٱلْحَيَوٰةُ ٱلدُّنْيَا ۖ وَلَا يَغُرَّنَّكُم بِٱللَّهِ ٱلْغَرُورُ
- 35:6 إِنَّ ٱلشَّيْطَٰنَ لَكُمْ عَدُوٌّۭ فَٱتَّخِذُوهُ عَدُوًّا ۚ إِنَّمَا يَدْعُوا۟ حِزْبَهُۥ لِيَكُونُوا۟ مِنْ أَصْحَٰبِ ٱلسَّعِيرِ
- 35:7 ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ لَهُمْ عَذَابٌۭ شَدِيدٌۭ ۖ وَٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَعَمِلُوا۟ ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ لَهُم مَّغْفِرَةٌۭ وَأَجْرٌۭ كَبِيرٌ
- 35:8 أَفَمَن زُيِّنَ لَهُۥ سُوٓءُ عَمَلِهِۦ فَرَءَاهُ حَسَنًۭا ۖ فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يُضِلُّ مَن يَشَآءُ وَيَهْدِى مَن يَشَآءُ ۖ فَلَا تَذْهَبْ نَفْسُكَ عَلَيْهِمْ حَسَرَٰتٍ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَلِيمٌۢ بِمَا يَصْنَعُونَ
- 35:9 وَٱللَّهُ ٱلَّذِىٓ أَرْسَلَ ٱلرِّيَٰحَ فَتُثِيرُ سَحَابًۭا فَسُقْنَٰهُ إِلَىٰ بَلَدٍۢ مَّيِّتٍۢ فَأَحْيَيْنَا بِهِ ٱلْأَرْضَ بَعْدَ مَوْتِهَا ۚ كَذَٰلِكَ ٱلنُّشُورُ
- 35:10 مَن كَانَ يُرِيدُ ٱلْعِزَّةَ فَلِلَّهِ ٱلْعِزَّةُ جَمِيعًا ۚ إِلَيْهِ يَصْعَدُ ٱلْكَلِمُ ٱلطَّيِّبُ وَٱلْعَمَلُ ٱلصَّٰلِحُ يَرْفَعُهُۥ ۚ وَٱلَّذِينَ يَمْكُرُونَ ٱلسَّيِّـَٔاتِ لَهُمْ عَذَابٌۭ شَدِيدٌۭ ۖ وَمَكْرُ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ هُوَ يَبُورُ
- 35:11 وَٱللَّهُ خَلَقَكُم مِّن تُرَابٍۢ ثُمَّ مِن نُّطْفَةٍۢ ثُمَّ جَعَلَكُمْ أَزْوَٰجًۭا ۚ وَمَا تَحْمِلُ مِنْ أُنثَىٰ وَلَا تَضَعُ إِلَّا بِعِلْمِهِۦ ۚ وَمَا يُعَمَّرُ مِن مُّعَمَّرٍۢ وَلَا يُنقَصُ مِنْ عُمُرِهِۦٓ إِلَّا فِى كِتَٰبٍ ۚ إِنَّ ذَٰلِكَ عَلَى ٱللَّهِ يَسِيرٌۭ
- 35:12 وَمَا يَسْتَوِى ٱلْبَحْرَانِ هَٰذَا عَذْبٌۭ فُرَاتٌۭ سَآئِغٌۭ شَرَابُهُۥ وَهَٰذَا مِلْحٌ أُجَاجٌۭ ۖ وَمِن كُلٍّۢ تَأْكُلُونَ لَحْمًۭا طَرِيًّۭا وَتَسْتَخْرِجُونَ حِلْيَةًۭ تَلْبَسُونَهَا ۖ وَتَرَى ٱلْفُلْكَ فِيهِ مَوَاخِرَ لِتَبْتَغُوا۟ مِن فَضْلِهِۦ وَلَعَلَّكُمْ تَشْكُرُونَ
- 35:13 يُولِجُ ٱلَّيْلَ فِى ٱلنَّهَارِ وَيُولِجُ ٱلنَّهَارَ فِى ٱلَّيْلِ وَسَخَّرَ ٱلشَّمْسَ وَٱلْقَمَرَ كُلٌّۭ يَجْرِى لِأَجَلٍۢ مُّسَمًّۭى ۚ ذَٰلِكُمُ ٱللَّهُ رَبُّكُمْ لَهُ ٱلْمُلْكُ ۚ وَٱلَّذِينَ تَدْعُونَ مِن دُونِهِۦ مَا يَمْلِكُونَ مِن قِطْمِيرٍ
- 35:14 إِن تَدْعُوهُمْ لَا يَسْمَعُوا۟ دُعَآءَكُمْ وَلَوْ سَمِعُوا۟ مَا ٱسْتَجَابُوا۟ لَكُمْ ۖ وَيَوْمَ ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ يَكْفُرُونَ بِشِرْكِكُمْ ۚ وَلَا يُنَبِّئُكَ مِثْلُ خَبِيرٍۢ
- 35:15 ۞ يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلنَّاسُ أَنتُمُ ٱلْفُقَرَآءُ إِلَى ٱللَّهِ ۖ وَٱللَّهُ هُوَ ٱلْغَنِىُّ ٱلْحَمِيدُ
- 35:16 إِن يَشَأْ يُذْهِبْكُمْ وَيَأْتِ بِخَلْقٍۢ جَدِيدٍۢ
- 35:17 وَمَا ذَٰلِكَ عَلَى ٱللَّهِ بِعَزِيزٍۢ
- 35:18 وَلَا تَزِرُ وَازِرَةٌۭ وِزْرَ أُخْرَىٰ ۚ وَإِن تَدْعُ مُثْقَلَةٌ إِلَىٰ حِمْلِهَا لَا يُحْمَلْ مِنْهُ شَىْءٌۭ وَلَوْ كَانَ ذَا قُرْبَىٰٓ ۗ إِنَّمَا تُنذِرُ ٱلَّذِينَ يَخْشَوْنَ رَبَّهُم بِٱلْغَيْبِ وَأَقَامُوا۟ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ ۚ وَمَن تَزَكَّىٰ فَإِنَّمَا يَتَزَكَّىٰ لِنَفْسِهِۦ ۚ وَإِلَى ٱللَّهِ ٱلْمَصِيرُ
- 35:19 وَمَا يَسْتَوِى ٱلْأَعْمَىٰ وَٱلْبَصِيرُ
- 35:20 وَلَا ٱلظُّلُمَٰتُ وَلَا ٱلنُّورُ
- 35:21 وَلَا ٱلظِّلُّ وَلَا ٱلْحَرُورُ
- 35:22 وَمَا يَسْتَوِى ٱلْأَحْيَآءُ وَلَا ٱلْأَمْوَٰتُ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يُسْمِعُ مَن يَشَآءُ ۖ وَمَآ أَنتَ بِمُسْمِعٍۢ مَّن فِى ٱلْقُبُورِ
- 35:23 إِنْ أَنتَ إِلَّا نَذِيرٌ
- 35:24 إِنَّآ أَرْسَلْنَٰكَ بِٱلْحَقِّ بَشِيرًۭا وَنَذِيرًۭا ۚ وَإِن مِّنْ أُمَّةٍ إِلَّا خَلَا فِيهَا نَذِيرٌۭ
- 35:25 وَإِن يُكَذِّبُوكَ فَقَدْ كَذَّبَ ٱلَّذِينَ مِن قَبْلِهِمْ جَآءَتْهُمْ رُسُلُهُم بِٱلْبَيِّنَٰتِ وَبِٱلزُّبُرِ وَبِٱلْكِتَٰبِ ٱلْمُنِيرِ
- 35:26 ثُمَّ أَخَذْتُ ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ ۖ فَكَيْفَ كَانَ نَكِيرِ
- 35:27 أَلَمْ تَرَ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ أَنزَلَ مِنَ ٱلسَّمَآءِ مَآءًۭ فَأَخْرَجْنَا بِهِۦ ثَمَرَٰتٍۢ مُّخْتَلِفًا أَلْوَٰنُهَا ۚ وَمِنَ ٱلْجِبَالِ جُدَدٌۢ بِيضٌۭ وَحُمْرٌۭ مُّخْتَلِفٌ أَلْوَٰنُهَا وَغَرَابِيبُ سُودٌۭ
- 35:28 وَمِنَ ٱلنَّاسِ وَٱلدَّوَآبِّ وَٱلْأَنْعَٰمِ مُخْتَلِفٌ أَلْوَٰنُهُۥ كَذَٰلِكَ ۗ إِنَّمَا يَخْشَى ٱللَّهَ مِنْ عِبَادِهِ ٱلْعُلَمَٰٓؤُا۟ ۗ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَزِيزٌ غَفُورٌ
- 35:29 إِنَّ ٱلَّذِينَ يَتْلُونَ كِتَٰبَ ٱللَّهِ وَأَقَامُوا۟ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ وَأَنفَقُوا۟ مِمَّا رَزَقْنَٰهُمْ سِرًّۭا وَعَلَانِيَةًۭ يَرْجُونَ تِجَٰرَةًۭ لَّن تَبُورَ
- 35:30 لِيُوَفِّيَهُمْ أُجُورَهُمْ وَيَزِيدَهُم مِّن فَضْلِهِۦٓ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ غَفُورٌۭ شَكُورٌۭ
- 35:31 وَٱلَّذِىٓ أَوْحَيْنَآ إِلَيْكَ مِنَ ٱلْكِتَٰبِ هُوَ ٱلْحَقُّ مُصَدِّقًۭا لِّمَا بَيْنَ يَدَيْهِ ۗ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ بِعِبَادِهِۦ لَخَبِيرٌۢ بَصِيرٌۭ
- 35:32 ثُمَّ أَوْرَثْنَا ٱلْكِتَٰبَ ٱلَّذِينَ ٱصْطَفَيْنَا مِنْ عِبَادِنَا ۖ فَمِنْهُمْ ظَالِمٌۭ لِّنَفْسِهِۦ وَمِنْهُم مُّقْتَصِدٌۭ وَمِنْهُمْ سَابِقٌۢ بِٱلْخَيْرَٰتِ بِإِذْنِ ٱللَّهِ ۚ ذَٰلِكَ هُوَ ٱلْفَضْلُ ٱلْكَبِيرُ
- 35:33 جَنَّٰتُ عَدْنٍۢ يَدْخُلُونَهَا يُحَلَّوْنَ فِيهَا مِنْ أَسَاوِرَ مِن ذَهَبٍۢ وَلُؤْلُؤًۭا ۖ وَلِبَاسُهُمْ فِيهَا حَرِيرٌۭ
- 35:34 وَقَالُوا۟ ٱلْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ ٱلَّذِىٓ أَذْهَبَ عَنَّا ٱلْحَزَنَ ۖ إِنَّ رَبَّنَا لَغَفُورٌۭ شَكُورٌ
- 35:35 ٱلَّذِىٓ أَحَلَّنَا دَارَ ٱلْمُقَامَةِ مِن فَضْلِهِۦ لَا يَمَسُّنَا فِيهَا نَصَبٌۭ وَلَا يَمَسُّنَا فِيهَا لُغُوبٌۭ
- 35:36 وَٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ لَهُمْ نَارُ جَهَنَّمَ لَا يُقْضَىٰ عَلَيْهِمْ فَيَمُوتُوا۟ وَلَا يُخَفَّفُ عَنْهُم مِّنْ عَذَابِهَا ۚ كَذَٰلِكَ نَجْزِى كُلَّ كَفُورٍۢ
- 35:37 وَهُمْ يَصْطَرِخُونَ فِيهَا رَبَّنَآ أَخْرِجْنَا نَعْمَلْ صَٰلِحًا غَيْرَ ٱلَّذِى كُنَّا نَعْمَلُ ۚ أَوَلَمْ نُعَمِّرْكُم مَّا يَتَذَكَّرُ فِيهِ مَن تَذَكَّرَ وَجَآءَكُمُ ٱلنَّذِيرُ ۖ فَذُوقُوا۟ فَمَا لِلظَّٰلِمِينَ مِن نَّصِيرٍ
- 35:38 إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَٰلِمُ غَيْبِ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ عَلِيمٌۢ بِذَاتِ ٱلصُّدُورِ
- 35:39 هُوَ ٱلَّذِى جَعَلَكُمْ خَلَٰٓئِفَ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ ۚ فَمَن كَفَرَ فَعَلَيْهِ كُفْرُهُۥ ۖ وَلَا يَزِيدُ ٱلْكَٰفِرِينَ كُفْرُهُمْ عِندَ رَبِّهِمْ إِلَّا مَقْتًۭا ۖ وَلَا يَزِيدُ ٱلْكَٰفِرِينَ كُفْرُهُمْ إِلَّا خَسَارًۭا
- 35:40 قُلْ أَرَءَيْتُمْ شُرَكَآءَكُمُ ٱلَّذِينَ تَدْعُونَ مِن دُونِ ٱللَّهِ أَرُونِى مَاذَا خَلَقُوا۟ مِنَ ٱلْأَرْضِ أَمْ لَهُمْ شِرْكٌۭ فِى ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ أَمْ ءَاتَيْنَٰهُمْ كِتَٰبًۭا فَهُمْ عَلَىٰ بَيِّنَتٍۢ مِّنْهُ ۚ بَلْ إِن يَعِدُ ٱلظَّٰلِمُونَ بَعْضُهُم بَعْضًا إِلَّا غُرُورًا
- 35:41 ۞ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يُمْسِكُ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضَ أَن تَزُولَا ۚ وَلَئِن زَالَتَآ إِنْ أَمْسَكَهُمَا مِنْ أَحَدٍۢ مِّنۢ بَعْدِهِۦٓ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ كَانَ حَلِيمًا غَفُورًۭا
- 35:42 وَأَقْسَمُوا۟ بِٱللَّهِ جَهْدَ أَيْمَٰنِهِمْ لَئِن جَآءَهُمْ نَذِيرٌۭ لَّيَكُونُنَّ أَهْدَىٰ مِنْ إِحْدَى ٱلْأُمَمِ ۖ فَلَمَّا جَآءَهُمْ نَذِيرٌۭ مَّا زَادَهُمْ إِلَّا نُفُورًا
- 35:43 ٱسْتِكْبَارًۭا فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ وَمَكْرَ ٱلسَّيِّئِ ۚ وَلَا يَحِيقُ ٱلْمَكْرُ ٱلسَّيِّئُ إِلَّا بِأَهْلِهِۦ ۚ فَهَلْ يَنظُرُونَ إِلَّا سُنَّتَ ٱلْأَوَّلِينَ ۚ فَلَن تَجِدَ لِسُنَّتِ ٱللَّهِ تَبْدِيلًۭا ۖ وَلَن تَجِدَ لِسُنَّتِ ٱللَّهِ تَحْوِيلًا
- 35:44 أَوَلَمْ يَسِيرُوا۟ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ فَيَنظُرُوا۟ كَيْفَ كَانَ عَٰقِبَةُ ٱلَّذِينَ مِن قَبْلِهِمْ وَكَانُوٓا۟ أَشَدَّ مِنْهُمْ قُوَّةًۭ ۚ وَمَا كَانَ ٱللَّهُ لِيُعْجِزَهُۥ مِن شَىْءٍۢ فِى ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَلَا فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ ۚ إِنَّهُۥ كَانَ عَلِيمًۭا قَدِيرًۭا
- 35:45 وَلَوْ يُؤَاخِذُ ٱللَّهُ ٱلنَّاسَ بِمَا كَسَبُوا۟ مَا تَرَكَ عَلَىٰ ظَهْرِهَا مِن دَآبَّةٍۢ وَلَٰكِن يُؤَخِّرُهُمْ إِلَىٰٓ أَجَلٍۢ مُّسَمًّۭى ۖ فَإِذَا جَآءَ أَجَلُهُمْ فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ كَانَ بِعِبَادِهِۦ بَصِيرًۢا


===== _commentary/v16/work/s035/surah.r2/dictionary.md =====
# Dictionary: every branch of every focus root

Every root of the surah's words, each once, headed by the ayah and word where it first occurs; it also serves the later words listed with it.
One entry per branch: a Turkish label and Turkish glosses of its attested senses, then the classical dictionaries' own phrases with their source tags (ayn, sihah, tahdhib, maqayis, mufradat, jamhara). [kalıp] marks a sense the dictionaries attest only inside a fixed expression.
Identity roots come from the quran-data gateway. Word-scoped alternatives are cited analyses of this
exact word. **Echo roots** are observed but withheld mappings: sound-family candidates, not identity.

  الحمد نقيض الذم (maqayis;ayn;jamhara;sihah;tahdhib)؛ الحمد الثناء (ayn;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ الحمد أعم من الشكر (sihah;mufradat)؛ التحميد كثرة حمد الله بحسن المحامد (ayn;tahdhib)

  أحمدت فلانا إذا وجدته محمودا (maqayis;ayn;sihah;tahdhib)؛ أحمدت الأرض إذا رضيت سكناها أو مرعاها (jamhara;sihah)؛ هل تحمد لي هذا الأمر أي هل ترضاه لي (tahdhib)
- **B003** övülen veya birçok övülesi niteliği bulunan kimse — övülen, övgüye değer · çokça övülen, birçok övülesi niteliği bulunan · övülmeye daha çok layık olan ya da daha çok öven · övülen; bağlama göre öven

- **B004** övülesi işin varılabilecek en ileri sınırı — yapabileceğinin en ilerisi ve övülecek olanı · aktarılan sözde kadınlarda övülebilecek niteliklerin en ileri derecesi
  حماداك أن تفعل كذا أي غايتك وفعلك المحمود (maqayis)؛ حماداك أن تفعل كذا أي حمدك (ayn;tahdhib)؛ حماداك في معنى قصاراك (jamhara;sihah)؛ حماديات النساء معناه غاية ما يحمد منهن (tahdhib)؛ حماداك أي غايتك المحمودة (mufradat)

  عمل الشيء صنعا (maqayis)؛ صنع يصنع صنعا وصنعته فهو صناعتي (ayn)؛ الصنع مصدر قولك صنع إليه معروفا وصنع به صنيعا قبيحا أي فعل (sihah)؛ صنع الله ذلك صنعا (tahdhib)؛ الصنع إجادة الفعل فكل صنع فعل (mufradat)
- **B002** el işi ustalığı ve meslek — el işçisi, bir işi yapan kişi · edinilmiş iş ve el uğraşı · ustalıkla yapılan iş veya ürün · elleriyle ustaca çalışan kadın · elleriyle ustaca çalışan erkek
  امرأة صناع ورجل صنع حاذقان فيما يصنعانه (maqayis)؛ الصناع الذين يعملون بأيديهم وامرأة صناع (ayn)؛ الصناعة حرفة الصانع وعمله الصنعة ورجل صنيع اليدين (sihah)؛ الصناع الذين يعملون بأيديهم والحرفة الصناعة وامرأة صناع ورجل صنع (tahdhib)؛ للحاذق المجيد صنع وللحاذقة المجيدة صناع (mufradat)
- **B003** birine iyilik etme — başkasına yapılan iyilik · ona iyilik yaptı · başkasına yardım etti
  الصنيعة ما اصطنعته من خير (maqayis)؛ الصنيعة ما اصطنعت من خير إلى غيرك (ayn)؛ صنع إليه معروفا واصطنعت عند فلان صنيعة (sihah)؛ الصنيعة ما أعطيته وأسديته من معروف وأصنع الرجل إذا أعان آخر (tahdhib)؛ الصنيعة ما اصطنعته من خير (mufradat)
- **B004** özenilmiş iyi görünüş sergileme — iyi görünmeye çalışma; yapmacıklık · kadın kendini süsledi
  التصنع حسن السمت (maqayis)؛ التصنع تكلف حسن السمت وتصنعت المرأة إذا صنعت نفسها (sihah)؛ التصنع تكلف حسن السمت وإظهاره والتزين به والباطن مدخول (tahdhib)
- **B005** özenle geliştirip iyi duruma getirme — iyi bakılıp beslenmiş at · parlatılıp iyi işlenmiş kılıç · birinin yetiştirip eğittiği kişi · onu kendim için yetiştirip hazırladım · gözetimim altında yetiştirilesin · iyi ve temiz dokunmuş giysi; özenli ürün
  فرس صنيع صنعة أهله بحسن القيام عليه (maqayis)؛ صنعة الفرس حسن القيام عليه وسيف صنيع أي مجلو واصطنعت فلانا لنفسي إذا اصطنعته وخرجته (sihah)؛ لتربى بمرأى مني وفلان صنيع فلان إذا رباه وأدبه وخرجه والصنيع الثوب الجيد النقي (tahdhib)؛ الاصطناع المبالغة في إصلاح الشيء وفرس صنيع أحسن القيام عليه (mufradat)
- **B006** su tutakları ve büyük yapılar — su tutakları; kaleler ve büyük yapılar · yağmur suyu toplayan havuz veya tutak · su tutakları · suyu bir süre tutan tahta düzenek
  المصانع ما يصنع من بئر وغيرها للسقى (maqayis)؛ المصنعة كالحوض يجمع فيه ماء المطر والمصانع الحصون (sihah)؛ المصانع الأبنية وأحباس الماء والقصور والقرى ومساكات لماء السماء (tahdhib)؛ عبر عن الأمكنة الشريفة بالمصانع (mufradat)
- **B007** çıkar vererek ya da yumuşak davranarak gönül alma — para vererek iş gördürme; gönül alma · yöneticiye para verdi veya onu yumuşaklıkla idare etti · ona uyum gösterdi ve yumuşak davrandı · bütün hızını vermeyip gücünü saklayan at
  المصانعة وهي كالرشوة (maqayis)؛ المصانعة الرشوة (sihah)؛ صانعت الوالي إذا راشيته وصانعته إذا داهنته وصانعت فلانا أي رافقته وفرس مصانع يصانعك ببذل سيره (tahdhib)؛ كني بالرشوة عن المصانعة (mufradat)
- **B008** şiş ya da şişte pişmiş et — et pişirme şişi veya şişte pişmiş et
  مما شذ عن هذا الأصل الصنع يقال إنه السفود (maqayis)؛ يكون الصنع الشواء والصنع السفود (tahdhib)
- **B009** babanla birlikte ne yaptın? [kalıp] — babanla birlikte ne yaptın?
  قولهم ما صنعت وأباك تقديره مع أبيك (sihah)
- **B010** çağrılı yemek ve bunun için yiyecek hazırlama — çağrılı yemek veya davetliler için hazırlanan yiyecek · vermek üzere yiyecek hazırladılar
  المصنعة الدعوة يتخذها الرجل ويدعو إخوانه إليها؛ اصطنعوا أي اتخذوا طعاما تنفقونه في سبيل الله (tahdhib)
- **B011** pazar yeri — pazar yerleri
  الأصناع الأسواق جمع صنع (tahdhib)

## ر و ح (root_000609): 35:9 ٱلرِّيَٰحَ

- **B001** bedene canlılık veren iç varlık — bedene yaşam veren ve ölümde ayrılan iç varlık
  الروح النفس التي يحيا بها البدن؛ خرجت روحه أي نفسه (ayn)؛ روح الإنسان مختلف فيه فقال قوم هي نفسه التي يقوم بها جسمه وقال آخرون الروح خلاف النفس (jamhara)؛ الروح يذكر ويؤنث والجمع الأرواح (sihah)؛ فالروح روح الإنسان وإنما هو مشتق من الريح (maqayis)
- **B002** kutsal bildiri veya göksel varlık adı — vahyi taşıyan göksel elçi veya kutsal bildiri için kullanılan ad · göksel varlıklara ilişkin veya canlılık taşıyan
  الروحاني من الخلق نحو الملائكة؛ الروح جبرئيل وهو روح القدس؛ الروح ملك يقوم وحده (ayn)؛ الروح الأمين جبريل؛ الروحانيون من الملائكة (jamhara)؛ يسمى القرآن روحا وكذلك جبريل وعيسى؛ روحانيون (sihah)؛ والروح جبرئيل عليه السلام (maqayis)
- **B003** hareket eden hava ve esinti — rüzgar · yel esintisi · yeli hoş gün · sert yelli gün · yelpaze · rüzgar geçiren açık yer
  الريح معروفة وأصل هذه الياء واو (jamhara)؛ الريح واحدة الرياح والأرياح؛ الروح نسيم الريح؛ يوم روح وريوح أي طيب؛ راح اليوم إذا اشتدت ريحه؛ المروحة ما يتروح بها والموضع الذي تخترق فيه الرياح (sihah)؛ أصل ذلك كله الريح؛ الروح نسيم الريح؛ ريح الغدير أصابته الريح؛ أراح القوم دخلوا في الريح؛ يوم ريح طيب ويوم راح ذو ريح شديدة؛ المروحة الموضع تخترق فيه الريح (maqayis)؛ فالريح معروفة (maqayis-ريح)
- **B004** koku ve kokudaki değişim — şeyin kokusu · burunla algılanan koku · et koktu · suyun kokusu değişti · kokulandırılmış yağ
  إرواح اللحم تغير ريحه (ayn)؛ مكان ريح أي طيب الروح (jamhara)؛ وجدت ريح الشيء ورائحته؛ الدهن المروح المطيب؛ أراح اللحم أي أنتن؛ أراح الشيء أي وجد ريحه؛ أروح الماء وغيره أي تغيرت ريحه؛ تروح الماء إذا أخذ ريح غيره؛ أروحت من فلان طيبا (sihah)؛ أروح الماء وغيره تغيرت رائحته؛ الدهن المروح المطيب؛ لم يرح رائحة الجنة؛ أروحني الصيد إذا وجد ريحك؛ أروحت من فلان طيبا (maqayis)
- **B005** geç gün vakti ve akşam dönüşü — öğle sonrası vakit ve bu vakitte gidiş · geç gün vaktinde yola çıktı · sürüyü akşam barınağa döndürdü · sürünün geceleme yeri
  الرواح من لدن زوال الشمس إلى الليل؛ السير والعمل بالعشي؛ تروح القوم في معنى راحوا؛ المراح الموضع؛ الإراحة رد الإبل بالعشي (ayn)؛ راح الرجل من رواح العشي؛ أراح ماشيته؛ الرواح الراحة أيضا (jamhara)؛ الرواح نقيض الصباح من زوال الشمس إلى الليل؛ سرحت الماشية بالغداة وراحت بالعشي؛ المراح حيث تأوي إليه الإبل والغنم؛ المراح الموضع الذي يروح منه القوم أو يروحون إليه (sihah)؛ الرواح العشي؛ راحوا في ذلك الوقت من لدن زوال الشمس إلى الليل؛ أرحنا إبلنا رددناها ذلك الوقت؛ المراح حيث تأوي الماشية بالليل (maqayis)
- **B006** hakkını kendisine geri vermek [kalıp] — onun hakkını kendisine geri verdim
  أرحت على الرجل حقه إذا رددته عليه (sihah)؛ أرحت على الرجل حقه إذا رددته إليه (maqayis)
- **B007** dinlenip güç toplama — dinlenme ve yorgunluğu giderme · soluklandı ve yorgunluktan toparlandı · kolaylık ve sıkıntısızlık · gereksinim giderme yeri · ona yaslanıp dinginleşti · gece ibadetinin her dört bölümlük dizisinden sonraki dinlenme
  ما لفلان في كذا من رواح أي من راحة (ayn)؛ الرواح الراحة أيضا؛ أرحت فلانا من كذا إراحة (jamhara)؛ الروح والراحة من الاستراحة؛ أراحه الله فاستراح؛ أراح الرجل رجعت إليه نفسه بعد الإعياء؛ أراح تنفس؛ افعل ذاك في سراح ورواح أي سهولة؛ استراح الرجل من الراحة؛ المستراح المخرج؛ استروح إليه أي استنام (sihah)؛ أراح الإنسان إذا تنفس؛ أراح الرجل إذا رجعت إليه نفسه بعد الإعياء؛ أفعل ذلك في سراح ورواح أي في سهولة؛ سميت الترويحة لاستراحة القوم (maqayis)
- **B008** iki seçenek arasında nöbetleşme — iki iş veya durum arasında nöbetleşme · ağırlığını sırayla iki bacağına verdi
  المراوحة عملان في عمل يعمل ذاك مرة وهذا مرة (ayn)؛ المراوحة في العملين أن يعمل هذا مرة وهذا مرة؛ راوح بين رجليه إذا قام على إحداهما مرة وعلى الأخرى مرة (sihah)؛ المراوحة في العملين أن يعمل هذا مرة وهذا مرة (maqayis)
- **B009** duyusal açıklık ve yayvan genişlik — ayakların ön bölümlerindeki açıklık · ayak uçları birbirinden açık duran · sığ ve geniş çanak
  رجل أروح في صدر قدمه انبساط؛ بعير أروح وقدم أروح وروحاء؛ قصعة روحاء قريبة القعر (ayn)؛ رجل أروح وامرأة روحاء وهو دون الفحج (jamhara)؛ الروح بالتحريك السعة؛ الروح أيضا سعة في الرجلين؛ قصعة روحاء أي قريبة القعر (sihah)؛ أصل كبير يدل على سعة وفسحة؛ الأروح الذي في صدور قدميه انبساط؛ قصعة روحاء قريبة القعر؛ لكل شيء واسع أريح (maqayis)
- **B010** iyiliğe hevesle yönelme — iyilik için gönüllü coşku ve geniş gönüllülük · iyilik yapmaya hevesle yöneldi
  راح فلان للمعروف يراح راحة إذا أخذته له خفة وأريحية؛ راحت يده بكذا؛ الراح الارتياح؛ الارتياح النشاط؛ الأريحي الواسع الخلق؛ أخذته الأريحية إذا ارتاح للندى (sihah)؛ يقال فلان يراح للمعروف إذا أخذته له أريحية؛ أريحي؛ الأريحي مأخوذ من راح يراح (maqayis)
- **B011** güç, üstünlük ve egemenlik — güç, üstünlük ve egemenlik
  قد تكون الريح بمعنى الغلبة والقوة؛ وتذهب ريحكم (sihah)؛ الريح الغلبة والقوة في قوله تعالى فتفشلوا وتذهب ريحكم (maqayis-ريح)
- **B012** kokulu bitki veya ekin yaprağı — kokulu bitki veya ekinin yaprağı
  الريحان نبت معروف؛ والحب ذو العصف والريحان فالعصف ساق الزرع والريحان ورقه (sihah)؛ الريحان معروف (maqayis-ريح)
- **B013** ferahlık ve geçim payı — ferahlık veya esirgeme ile geçim payı · yaratıcıdan gelen veya istenen geçim payı
  روح وريحان؛ الروح الراحة والريحان الرزق (jamhara)؛ روح وريحان أي رحمة ورزق؛ الريحان الرزق؛ خرجت أبتغي ريحان الله؛ سبحان الله وريحانه يريدون استرزاقا (sihah)؛ الريحان الرزق؛ الولد من ريحان الله (maqayis-ريح)
- **B014** avuç içi — avuç içi · avuç içleri
  راحة الإنسان معروفة والجمع راح (jamhara)؛ الراح جمع راحة وهي الكف (sihah)؛ الراح جماعة راحة الكف (maqayis)
- **B015** üzümden yapılan sarhoş edici içki — üzümden yapılan sarhoş edici içki
  الرياح بالفتح الراح وهي الخمر؛ الراح الخمر (sihah)؛ الراح الخمر (maqayis)
- **B016** öldü [kalıp] — adam öldü
  أراح الرجل أي مات (sihah)؛ يقال للميت إذا قضى قد أراح (maqayis)
- **B017** ağacın yapraklanması veya bitkinin uzaması [kalıp] — ağaç yaz sonrasında yeniden yapraklandı · bitki boy attı
  راح الشجر يراح مثل تروح أي تفطر بورق؛ تروح الشجر إذا تفطر بورق بعد إدبار الصيف؛ تروح النبت أي طال (sihah)؛ تروح الشجر وراح يراح معناهما أن يتفطر بالورق (maqayis)
- **B018** erkek atın damızlık olgunluğa erişmesi [kalıp] — erkek at damızlık olgunluğa erişti
  راح الفرس يراح راحة إذا تحصن أي صار فحلا (sihah)؛ وراح الفرس يراح راحة إذا تحصن (maqayis)
- **B019** dağılan veya yuvasına dönen kuşlar — 
  الروح في هذا البيت المتفرقة (ayn)؛ طير روح أي متفرقة؛ وقيل هي الرائحة إلى مواضعها (sihah)؛ قال قوم هي المتفرقة وقال آخرون هي الرائحة إلى أوكارها (maqayis)

## ث و ر (root_000210): 35:9 فَتُثِيرُ

- **B001** gizlilikten çıkıp belirerek yayılma — durgunluktan çıkıp belirmek ve yayılmak · tozun veya bulutun yükselip yayılması · hastalık döküntüsünün ortaya çıkıp yayılması · böcek sürüsünün saklandığı yerden çıkıp sıçrayarak yayılması · alacakaranlığın yayılması veya en yoğun bölümünün görünmesi · içi kalkmak · saçı dağılıp kabarmış
  أصل انبعاث الشيء (maqayis)؛ ثار الغبار والسحاب ونحوهما انتشر ساطعا (mufradat)؛ ثار الغبار يثور ثورا وثورانا أي سطع (sihah)؛ ثارت الحصبة... وثار الجراد... وثار الماء... وثار الغبار وغيره كذلك (jamhara)
- **B002** yerinden kaldırıp harekete geçirme — bir şeyi yerinden oynatıp harekete geçirmek · toprağı eşeleyip kaldırmak · erkek sığırın toprağı ayaklarıyla eşeleyip kaldırması · tavşanı ürkütüp saklandığı yerden çıkarmak · Kur'an bilgisini derinlemesine araştırıp ortaya çıkarmak · rahatsız edip yerinden kaldırmak
  أثرت الأرض إثارة (jamhara)؛ أثار الثور التراب إذا بحثه بقوائمه (jamhara)؛ وأثاره غيره (sihah)؛ وثور القرآن أي بحث عن علمه (sihah)؛ فتثير سحابا... وأثاروا الأرض (mufradat)
- **B003** saldırgan biçimde kabarıp karşı koyma — birinin üzerine atılıp saldırmak · halkın birinin üzerine ayaklanması · kötülüğü kışkırtıp onlara karşı ortaya çıkarmak · öfkesi kabarıp dışa vurulmak · taşkınlık ve kargaşa
  ثاور فلان فلانا إذا واثبه (maqayis;jamhara)؛ ثار به الناس أي وثبوا عليه (sihah)؛ المثاورة المواثبة (sihah)؛ انتظر حتى تسكن هذه الثورة وهي الهيج (sihah)؛ ثور فلان عليهم الشرا أي هيجه وأظهره (sihah)؛ ثار ثائره كناية عن انتشار غضبه (mufradat)
- **B004** erkek sığır — yabani veya evcil erkek sığır · dişi sığır · erkek sığırlar · sığırların sudan kaçınması üzerine söylenen, hayvan ya da yosun diye yorumlanan örnek söz
  جنس من الحيوان (maqayis)؛ والثور ذكر البقر الوحشية والأهلية (jamhara)؛ والثور الذكر من البقر والأنثى ثورة (sihah)؛ والثور البقر الذي يثار به الأرض (mufradat)
- **B005** kurutulmuş çökelek parçası — kurutulmuş çökelekten bir parça, özellikle iri bir parça
  الثور فالقطعة من الأقط (maqayis)؛ والثور القطعة العظيمة من الأقط والجمع أثوار وثورة (jamhara)؛ والثور قطعة من الأقط والجمع ثورة (sihah)
- **B006** dağ, topluluk veya burç için özel ad — belirli bir dağın adı · belirli bir boyun veya kabilenin adı · gökteki belirli bir burcun adı
  وثور جبل وثور قوم من العرب وهذا على التشبيه (maqayis)؛ والثور جبل معروف يسمى ثور أطحل وبنو ثور بطن من الرباب (jamhara)؛ وثور أبو قبيلة من مضر وثور جبل بمكة... والثور برج من السماء (sihah)
- **B007** su yüzeyini kaplayan yosun — su yüzeyine çıkıp tabaka oluşturan yosun · sığırların sudan kaçınması üzerine söylenen, hayvan ya da yosun diye yorumlanan örnek söz
  الثور فيمن يقول إنه الطحلب... ثار على متن الماء (maqayis)؛ يقال للطحلب ثور الماء (sihah)

## ECHO ء ث ر (root_000011): for 35:9 فَتُثِيرُ: withheld observed target; not identity

- **B001** en başa almak; yapmaya kesin karar vermek [kalıp] — her şeyden önce · ilk iş olarak · yapmaya kesin karar verdi
  له أصل تقديم الشيء (maqayis)؛ آثرا ما وآثر ذي أثير أي أول كل شيء (maqayis;sihah;tahdhib)؛ إن آثرت أن تأتينا فأتنا وقد أثر أن يفعل ذلك الأمر أي فرغ له وعزم عليه (tahdhib)؛ خذه آثرا ما وإثرا ما وأثر ذي أثير (mufradat)

  العجز مؤخر الشيء والجمع أعجاز (maqayis;ayn;sihah)؛ عجز الإنسان مؤخره وبه شبه مؤخر غيره (mufradat)؛ أعجاز الإبل مآخيرها (tahdhib)؛ عجز الأمر وأعجاز الأمور (maqayis)؛ العجيزة عجيزة المرأة خاصة (maqayis;ayn;sihah;tahdhib)؛ امرأة عجزاء عظيمة العجز (maqayis;ayn;jamhara;sihah;tahdhib)؛ تعجزت البعير ركبت عجزه (sihah;tahdhib)؛ العجازة ما تعظم به المرأة عجيزتها (jamhara;sihah;tahdhib)

  العجزاء من الرمل رملة مرتفعة كأنها جبل (maqayis)؛ العجزاء من الرمل خاصة رملة مرتفعة كأنها جبل ليس بركام رمل وهي مكرمة المنبت (ayn)؛ العجزاء رملة مرتفعة (sihah)؛ العجوز رملة بالدهناء (sihah)؛ العجزاء من الرمال حبل مرتفع كأنه جلد ليس بركام رمل وهو مكرمة للنبت (tahdhib)

  العجز داء يأخذ الدابة في عجزها (maqayis;ayn)؛ العجزاء من العقبان الخفيفة العجيزة (maqayis)؛ عقاب عجزاء اختلفوا في تفسيره (jamhara)؛ إذا كان في ذنبها ريشة بيضاء أو ريشتان (jamhara;tahdhib)؛ عقاب عجزاء للقصيرة الذنب (sihah)؛ العجزاء الشديدة الدابرة والشديدة الكف (jamhara)؛ يقال لدابرة الطائر العجازة (tahdhib)

## ك س ب (root_001296): 35:45 كَسَبُوا۟

- **B001** kendisi için geçimlik ya da yarar arayıp elde etme — geçimlik ve yarar arayıp elde etme · bir şeyi ya da parayı kendisi için kazanmak · bir şeyi özellikle kendisi için edinmek · kazanç sağlamak için uğraşmak · çok kazanan ya da geçimini arayan kimse · kişinin kazandığı şey ya da kazanç yolu · iyi ve temiz kazanç · para kazanan; ayrıca kurt ya da dişi köpek adı olarak kullanılan biçim

- **B020** iki katmanı üst üste getirmek [kalıp] — iki giysiyi veya iki zırhı üst üste getirmek
  ظاهر بين ثوبين أي طارق بينهما وطابق (sihah)؛ ظاهر فلان بين ثوبين وبين درعين إذا طابق بينهما (tahdhib)
- **B021** yedek hazırlayıp güvence sağlamak — gerektiğinde kullanılmak üzere hazır tutulan deve · yedek hazırlayarak önlem almak ve güvence sağlamak
  البعير الظهري العدة للحاجة (sihah;tahdhib)؛ الاستظهار في كلامهم الاحتياط والاستيثاق (tahdhib)؛ استظهر ببعيرين ظهريين محتاطا بهما (tahdhib)
- **B022** birbirine sırt çevirip uzaklaşmak — birbirine sırt çevirip uzaklaşmak
  تظاهر القوم إذا تدابروا (maqayis;sihah)؛ كل واحد منهما أدبر عن صاحبه وجعل ظهره إليه (maqayis)
- **B023** karşılıksız veya artandan vermek [kalıp] — karşılık beklemeden, kendiliğinden vermek · geçim gereklerinden artan bolluktan vermek
  عن ظهر يد معناه ابتداء من غير مكافأة (tahdhib)؛ ما كان عن ظهر غنى عن فضل عيال (tahdhib)
- **B024** bir şeyle övünmek [kalıp] — bir şeyle övünmek ve onu övünç dayanağı yapmak
  ظهرت به أي افتخرت به (tahdhib)؛ واظهر ببزته أي افخر به على غيره (tahdhib)



===== _commentary/v16/work/s035/surah.r2/channels.md =====
(source: latent_activation/network/v3/reviews/s035/reader_a_pilot.md)

# s035 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. P1 (35:1-14) Aperture, Containment, and Flow
- Semantic invariant: Matter or force becomes effective through a controlled relation between opening, channel, boundary, and release.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:2 `يَفْتَحِ/يُمْسِكْ`, 35:9 `تُثِيرُ/أَرْسَلَ`, 35:12 `ٱلْفُلْكَ ... مَوَاخِرَ`, and 35:13 `يَجْرِى`.
- Surprising reach: Mercy, rain, and maritime motion acquire a concrete hydraulic architecture of apertures, water-holding cavities, irrigated ground, and channels cut by moving water.

#### Subchannel A. Opening into release
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A closure splits or widens, a latent content erupts, and what was folded or confined spreads into an open course.
- Active motifs: opened closure (quranic:root_001124:B001/m01); water issuing from an outlet (quranic:root_001124:B005/m01); split or fissure (quranic:root_001165:B001/m01); unfolding and branching spread (quranic:root_001503:B001/m01); eruption from quiescence (quranic:root_000210:B001/m01); widening until flow becomes possible (quranic:root_001559:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:1 `فَاطِرِ` (ف ط ر); 35:2 `يَفْتَحِ` (ف ت ح); 35:9 `تُثِيرُ/ٱلنُّشُورُ` (ث و ر/ن ش ر); 35:13 `ٱلنَّهَارِ` (ن ه ر).
- Synthesis: The opening of mercy is materialized as a breached closure whose contents emerge, spread, and acquire a widened outlet. The resurrection root at 35:9 participates not only in revival but in the physical act of unfolding what had been closed.

#### Subchannel B. Reservoir, well, and irrigated ground
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water is caught in a depression or rock cavity, retained by a firm boundary, then conducted through soil until the ground is fit.
- Active motifs: water-gathering low ground (quranic:root_000086:B006/m01); rock cavity or newly dug well (quranic:root_000434:B011/m01); water-retaining site (quranic:root_001424:B004/m01); river cutting a channel (quranic:root_001559:B001/m01); water sent across soil to improve it (quranic:root_001405:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:1, 35:3, 35:11 `خَلْقِ/خَٰلِقٍ/خَلَقَ` (خ ل ق); 35:2 `مُمْسِكَ/يُمْسِكْ` (م س ك); 35:12 `بَحْرَانِ/مَوَاخِرَ` (ب ح ر/م خ ر); 35:13 `ٱلنَّهَارِ` (ن ه ر).
- Synthesis: Retention and release become one irrigation mechanism: a firm cavity first keeps water from vanishing, while a cut channel later distributes it through the land. This gives the opening and withholding of 35:2 a material analogue in managed water.

#### Subchannel C. Ship cleaving a moving medium
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A vessel enters flowing water, cuts it under way, and is managed by a water-skilled operator.
- Active motifs: continuous flowing motion (quranic:root_000240:B001/m01); ship circulating on water (quranic:root_001177:B003/m01); hull cleaving water with motion and sound (quranic:root_001405:B001/m01); sailor and navigation craft (quranic:root_001442:B006/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:12 `ٱلْفُلْكَ/مَوَاخِرَ/مِلْحٌ` (ف ل ك/م خ ر/م ل ح); 35:13 `يَجْرِى` (ج ر ي).
- Synthesis: The visible ships of 35:12 form a complete transport scene: current, vessel, cleaving operation, and operator. The vessel does not merely occupy the sea; it converts resistance into a traversable course.

### 2. P1 (35:1-14) Atmospheric Delivery and Vegetal Renewal
- Semantic invariant: A mobile atmospheric carrier delivers water that reverses terrestrial dormancy into growth and yield.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:9 explicitly sends wind, raises cloud, and revives dead land, while 35:3 and 35:12 frame rain and produce as provision.
- Surprising reach: The resurrection analogy expands into fine-grained plant processes: water-laden cloud mass, returning green in dry herbage, bursting shoots, and tree growth after seasonal recession.

#### Subchannel A. Wind, cloud mass, and rain-bearing transport
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Wind moves through the air, draws a cloud body into view, and carries a layered load whose descent functions as provision.
- Active motifs: wind and moving air (quranic:root_000609:B003/m01); trailing cloud (quranic:root_000680:B002/m01); water-bearing cloud load (quranic:root_000357:B010/m01); cloud emergence and clearing sky (quranic:root_000400:B005/m01); layered cloud mass (quranic:root_000532:B008/m01); rain descending as provision (quranic:root_000560:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:3 `يَرْزُقُ` (ر ز ق); 35:9 `ٱلرِّيَٰحَ/سَحَابًا` (ر و ح/س ح ب); 35:11 `تَحْمِلُ` (ح م ل); 35:12 `تَسْتَخْرِجُ` (خ ر ج); 35:13 `رَبُّ` (ر ب ب).
- Synthesis: Wind supplies motion, the cloud supplies a carried body, and rain supplies the delivered resource. The cloud is lexically a load and a layered mass, making atmospheric transport structurally parallel to the surah's other acts of sending and carrying.

#### Subchannel B. Dead ground returning to yield
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Unproductive ground receives rain, dry herbage greens again, tender shoots emerge, and the revived land produces a crop.
- Active motifs: soft fertile ground (quranic:root_000025:B002/m01); land's life through rain and vegetation (quranic:root_000383:B002/m01); dead unused land (quranic:root_001454:B003/m01); dry herbage revived by rain (quranic:root_001503:B004/m01); tender shoot emergence (quranic:root_000810:B004/m01); trees breaking into leaf and plants lengthening (quranic:root_000609:B017/m01); crop and tree yield (quranic:root_000043:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:1, 35:3, 35:9 `أَرْضِ/أَرْضَ` (ء ر ض); 35:5, 35:9 `حَيَوٰةُ/أَحْيَيْ` (ح ي ي); 35:9 `مَّيِّتٍ/مَوْتِ/ٱلنُّشُورُ/ٱلرِّيَٰحَ` (م و ت/ن ش ر/ر و ح); 35:12 `تَشْكُرُ/تَأْكُلُ` (ش ك ر/ء ك ل).
- Synthesis: Revival is staged as a causal sequence rather than a generic fertility image: dead ground, rain contact, regreening, shoot emergence, and edible yield. The result makes resurrection legible through the mechanics of seasonal plant return.

### 3. P1 (35:1-14) Formation and Fixation
- Semantic invariant: An intended form is brought into being and then stabilized as an artifact, inscription, rule, or record.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:1 and 35:11 name making and creation, while 35:11 fixes lifespan in a `كِتَٰب`.
- Surprising reach: Creation and writing converge as two kinds of form-making: one shapes beings, while the other arranges marks and makes an order durable.

#### Subchannel A. Origination as deliberate making
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An agent initiates, makes, and brings a formed entity into existence.
- Active motifs: making or producing a thing (quranic:root_000248:B001/m01); creative bringing-into-being (quranic:root_000434:B002/m01); performed manufacture (quranic:root_000885:B001/m01); origination and first emergence (quranic:root_001165:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:1 `فَاطِرِ/جَاعِلِ/خَلْقِ` (ف ط ر/ج ع ل/خ ل ق); 35:3 `خَٰلِقٍ` (خ ل ق); 35:8 `يَصْنَعُ` (ص ن ع); 35:11 `خَلَقَ/جَعَلَ` (خ ل ق/ج ع ل).
- Synthesis: The motifs distinguish initiation, fabrication, and emergence while keeping them in one production scene. Creation appears as the controlled establishment of form, not only as an abstract assertion of existence.

#### Subchannel B. Inscription, decree, and roster
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Marks are arranged into writing, writing fixes a binding determination, and names can be entered into a durable register.
- Active motifs: ordered letters and written artifact (quranic:root_001283:B002/m01); binding written decree (quranic:root_001283:B003/m01); enrollment in a register (quranic:root_001283:B004/m01); published or unsealed writing (quranic:root_001503:B009/m01); retraced inscription or tattoo lines (quranic:root_000544:B009/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:4 `تُرْجَعُ` (ر ج ع); 35:9 `ٱلنُّشُورُ` (ن ش ر); 35:11 `كِتَٰبٍ` (ك ت ب).
- Synthesis: The book of 35:11 activates several operations that should remain distinct: composing signs, fixing a decree, and entering a name in a roster. Retraced lines add the material act by which a mark is renewed so that its determination remains visible.

### 4. P1 (35:1-14) Reception and Report
- Semantic invariant: Meaning moves from disclosure through perception into response, report, or distortion.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:3 calls for recollection, 35:8 contrasts seeing and knowledge, 35:10 raises good speech, and 35:14 stages a call that is neither heard nor answered.
- Surprising reach: The scene includes both receptive organs and communication logistics: insight opens an obstruction, news is broadcast, speech returns as an answer, and falsehood is a report diverted from its proper direction.

#### Subchannel A. Sight becoming inward knowledge
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A visible object is perceived, reflected upon, and penetrated until its inner condition becomes known.
- Active motifs: sight by eye or insight (quranic:root_000531:B001/m01); reflective judgment of the heart (quranic:root_000531:B002/m01); disclosure to the knower (quranic:root_001040:B001/m01); knowledge of a report's interior (quranic:root_000387:B001/m01); accurate wisdom (quranic:root_000348:B003/m01); opening an intellectual obstruction (quranic:root_001124:B008/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:2 `حَكِيمُ/يَفْتَحِ` (ح ك م/ف ت ح); 35:8 `رَءَا/عَلِيمٌ` (ر ء ي/ع ل م); 35:11 `عِلْمِ` (ع ل م); 35:12 `تَرَى` (ر ء ي); 35:14 `خَبِيرٍ` (خ ب ر).
- Synthesis: Seeing is not the endpoint. The scene moves from visual contact to reflection, accurate judgment, and knowledge of what lies beneath the report's exterior, with opening serving as the mechanism that makes insight possible.

#### Subchannel B. Call, hearing, and returned answer
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A caller directs speech outward; a recipient hears, understands, and causes another to hear; an answer returns along the same communicative path.
- Active motifs: voiced call or summons (quranic:root_000478:B001/m01); auditory reception (quranic:root_000741:B001/m01); understanding and compliance after hearing (quranic:root_000741:B003/m01); causing another to hear (quranic:root_000741:B004/m01); verbal answer (quranic:root_000273:B003/m01); speech returned to its sender (quranic:root_000544:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:4 `تُرْجَعُ` (ر ج ع); 35:6, 35:13-14 `يَدْعُوا۟/تَدْعُ/دُعَآءَ` (د ع و); 35:14 `يَسْمَعُ/سَمِعُ/ٱسْتَجَابُ` (س م ع/ج و ب).
- Synthesis: The failed appeal to the invoked beings is measured against a complete response circuit: call, reception, comprehension, induced hearing, and returned speech. The surface denial removes each functional stage of that circuit.

#### Subchannel C. News circulated or turned into falsehood
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A meaningful utterance is carried and publicized, but it may be diverted from truth or used to mark its bearer as a liar.
- Active motifs: meaningful word or utterance (quranic:root_001316:B002/m01); reported news (quranic:root_001464:B002/m01); broadcasting a report (quranic:root_000582:B005/m01); mention carried on the tongue (quranic:root_000516:B004/m01); lie turned away from truth (quranic:root_000041:B002/m01); attribution of lying (quranic:root_001290:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:3 `تُؤْفَكُ/ٱذْكُرُ` (ء ف ك/ذ ك ر); 35:4 `يُكَذِّبُ/كُذِّبَتْ` (ك ذ ب); 35:10 `كَلِمُ/يَرْفَعُ` (ك ل م/ر ف ع); 35:14 `يُنَبِّئُ` (ن ب ء).
- Synthesis: Communication is a transport scene whose cargo can remain true or be redirected. Broadcasting raises a report into public circulation, while lying is lexically a deflection that bends the report away from its proper referent.

### 5. P1 (35:1-14) Mediating Surfaces
- Semantic invariant: A surface controls how an entity appears, what it exposes, and what it protects.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:8 presents an ugly deed made to look fair, while 35:12 presents extracted ornament and worn covering.
- Surprising reach: Appearance is grounded in craft and material layers: cosmetic display, armor, veil, hairy hide, leather container, and the stripping of flesh from bone.

#### Subchannel A. Crafted beauty and disfigurement
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A visible surface is judged, polished, or staged so that it appears attractive rather than defective.
- Active motifs: beauty opposed to ugliness (quranic:root_000323:B001/m01); ugliness and bad form (quranic:root_000755:B001/m01); beauty free of blemish (quranic:root_000660:B001/m01); active beautification (quranic:root_000660:B002/m01); self-fashioning and cosmetic display (quranic:root_000885:B004/m01); visible aspect and mirror-image (quranic:root_000531:B006/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:8 `حَسَنًا/سُوٓءُ/زُيِّنَ/رَءَا/يَصْنَعُ` (ح س ن/س و ء/ز ي ن/ر ء ي/ص ن ع); 35:10 `سَّيِّـَٔاتِ` (س و ء); 35:12 `تَرَى` (ر ء ي).
- Synthesis: The scene distinguishes intrinsic appraisal, deliberate enhancement, and the beholder's visible impression. The beautified evil of 35:8 is therefore not merely misjudged; it is lexically compatible with a crafted presentation designed to manage the viewer.

#### Subchannel B. Cover, veil, and armor
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A layer is placed over a vulnerable body or object to conceal, preserve, or defend it.
- Active motifs: protective covering (quranic:root_001096:B001/m01); covering over object or weapon (quranic:root_001307:B001/m01); bodily garment (quranic:root_001341:B001/m01); armor worn for defense (quranic:root_001341:B002/m01); tent-like fine screen (quranic:root_001315:B006/m01); garment or shield (quranic:root_000273:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:1, 35:12-13 `كُلِّ/كُلٍّ/كُلٌّ` (ك ل ل); 35:7, 35:14 `كَفَرُ/يَكْفُرُ` (ك ف ر); 35:7 `مَّغْفِرَةٌ` (غ ف ر); 35:12 `تَلْبَسُ` (ل ب س); 35:14 `ٱسْتَجَابُ` (ج و ب).
- Synthesis: Moral covering, ordinary dress, and martial defense share the operation of interposing a layer. The channel gives forgiveness and concealment a concrete protective geometry without collapsing them into the same surface reading.

#### Subchannel C. Hide converted into leather
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A marked animal surface loses hair or flesh, is stripped, and becomes a skin capable of holding contents.
- Active motifs: mark left on skin (quranic:root_000148:B006/m01); hide retaining hair during dressing (quranic:root_000844:B006/m01); skin that holds what is inside (quranic:root_001424:B005/m01); flesh stripped from bone (quranic:root_001348:B009/m01); shedding feather, hair, or old coat (quranic:root_000320:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:2 `مُمْسِكَ/يُمْسِكْ` (م س ك); 35:6 `أَصْحَٰبِ` (ص ح ب); 35:8 `حَسَرَٰتٍ` (ح س ر); 35:9 `بَلَدٍ` (ب ل د); 35:12 `لَحْمًا` (ل ح م).
- Synthesis: The surface becomes a worked material through shedding, stripping, and retention. This scene sharpens the distinction between living covering and manufactured container while keeping both within one conversion process.

### 6. P1 (35:1-14) Generative Bodies
- Semantic invariant: Generation proceeds through sexed bodies, an interior receptacle, gestational change, and eventual emergence.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:11 names female pregnancy and delivery after the creation of human pairs.
- Surprising reach: The human sequence is mirrored by a complete livestock scene of estrus, stallion display, threatened mounting, uncertain conception, and visible pregnancy.

#### Subchannel A. Human gestation and birth
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A female body receives and carries an interior growth in the womb until birth and postpartum emergence.
- Active motifs: female sex (quranic:root_000058:B001/m01); interior pregnancy or fruit (quranic:root_000357:B002/m01); womb as the child's growing receptacle (quranic:root_000552:B003/m01); delivery of the carried fetus (quranic:root_001657:B002/m01); birth and postpartum blood (quranic:root_001533:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:2 `رَّحْمَةٍ` (ر ح م); 35:8 `نَفْسُ` (ن ف س); 35:11 `أُنثَىٰ/تَحْمِلُ/تَضَعُ` (ء ن ث/ح م ل/و ض ع).
- Synthesis: The surface statement is expanded into a role-complete bodily process: female participant, womb-container, carried growth, delivery, and postpartum outcome. The pregnancy motif also preserves its plant-fruit extension without merging the two entities.

#### Subchannel B. Livestock courtship and uncertain conception
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A female desires mating, a stallion rises and bellows before mounting, and later signs either confirm pregnancy or disclose failed conception.
- Active motifs: female animal seeking the male (quranic:root_000248:B009/m01); stallion rising over females (quranic:root_000745:B003/m01); stallion's warning bellow before attack (quranic:root_001662:B005/m01); pregnancy becoming visibly apparent in ewe or camel (quranic:root_000531:B010/m01); suspected pregnancy that fails or is lost (quranic:root_000544:B013/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:1 `جَاعِلِ/سَّمَٰوَٰتِ` (ج ع ل/س م و); 35:4 `تُرْجَعُ` (ر ج ع); 35:5 `وَعْدَ` (و ع د); 35:8, 35:12 `رَءَا/تَرَى` (ر ء ي).
- Synthesis: The lexical evidence forms a distinct animal reproductive sequence rather than an undifferentiated fertility theme. Display, threat, mating readiness, and uncertain gestation make generation vulnerable to failure as well as productive completion.

### 7. P1 (35:1-14) Verticalized Power
- Semantic invariant: Upward motion becomes a model for increased rank, effective strength, and sovereign command.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:10 makes good speech ascend and righteous action raise it, while 35:1, 35:2, 35:10, and 35:13 name power, might, and dominion.
- Surprising reach: Physical elevation and political hierarchy are linked by a shared upward operation, but remain distinct scenes of movement and rule.

#### Subchannel A. Ascent and increasing height
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A body or utterance rises, gains height, and continues upward by increments.
- Active motifs: elevation and upwardness (quranic:root_000745:B001/m01); ascent toward a higher place (quranic:root_000862:B001/m01); increase in height or measure (quranic:root_000862:B008/m01); lifting a body from its place (quranic:root_000582:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:1, 35:3, 35:13 `سَّمَٰوَٰتِ/سَّمَآءِ/مُّسَمًّى` (س م و); 35:10 `يَصْعَدُ/يَرْفَعُ` (ص ع د/ر ف ع).
- Synthesis: The upward route of good speech is materially continuous with bodily ascent and incremental height. The process separates the motion itself from the status that the motion can later signify.

#### Subchannel B. Rank, dominance, and dominion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An elevated position becomes recognized rank, coercive advantage, effective capacity, and rule over a domain.
- Active motifs: elevation of rank (quranic:root_000582:B002/m01); strength and honor after lowliness (quranic:root_001008:B001/m01); dominance over a rival (quranic:root_001008:B002/m01); kingship and political dominion (quranic:root_001444:B003/m01); effective capacity to act and own (quranic:root_001205:B003/m01); lordship, ownership, and command (quranic:root_000532:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:1 `قَدِيرٌ/مَلَٰٓئِكَةِ` (ق د ر/م ل ك); 35:2, 35:10 `عَزِيزُ/عِزَّةَ` (ع ز ز); 35:10 `يَرْفَعُ` (ر ف ع); 35:13 `رَبُّ/مُلْكُ/يَمْلِكُ` (ر ب ب/م ل ك).
- Synthesis: Rank is not treated as a synonym for ascent. It is the social and political outcome produced when elevation becomes capacity, victory, ownership, and stable command.

### 8. P1 (35:1-14) Provision in Circulation
- Semantic invariant: Value remains productive when goods, payment, and assigned shares circulate; blockage turns circulation into stagnation and loss.
- Surface relation: indirect; 35:3 names assigned provision, 35:7 names reward, 35:10 says the plot will perish, and 35:12 depicts extraction and travel in pursuit of bounty.
- Surprising reach: The moral economy is rendered through a literal marketplace with pricing, sale, circulation, wage, and market stagnation.

#### Subchannel A. Market movement and stagnation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Goods are brought to a market, priced, and made current; when circulation fails, the market and its stock stagnate toward spoilage.
- Active motifs: market stagnation leading to spoilage (quranic:root_000164:B002/m01); market price (quranic:root_000708:B003/m01); marketplace for sale and purchase (quranic:root_000762:B005/m01); a market becoming current (quranic:root_001273:B018/m01); a sale reversed and replaced (quranic:root_000544:B011/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:4 `تُرْجَعُ` (ر ج ع); 35:6 `ٱلسَّعِيرِ` (س ع ر); 35:9 `سُقْ` (س و ق); 35:10 `يَبُورُ` (ب و ر); 35:14 `ٱلْقِيَٰمَةِ` (ق و م).
- Synthesis: Perishing is specified as failed circulation: unsold goods cease moving and decay. Pricing, market space, and reversed sale turn the fate of the plot in 35:10 into an economic mechanism rather than a bare synonym for destruction.

#### Subchannel B. Wage, assigned share, and continuing yield
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Work generates an agreed payment, provision is apportioned, and a continuing grant returns benefit to its recipient.
- Active motifs: wage or recompense for work (quranic:root_000015:B001/m01); fee fixed for a task (quranic:root_000248:B005/m01); apportioned provision (quranic:root_000560:B001/m01); worker's wage or ration (quranic:root_001046:B004/m01); continuing grant or provision (quranic:root_000240:B006/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:1, 35:11 `جَاعِلِ/جَعَلَ` (ج ع ل); 35:3 `يَرْزُقُ` (ر ز ق); 35:7 `أَجْرٌ/عَمِلُ` (ء ج ر/ع م ل); 35:8, 35:10 `عَمَلِ/عَمَلُ` (ع م ل); 35:13 `يَجْرِى` (ج ر ي).
- Synthesis: Reward is a structured return on action: a task, a fixed fee, an apportioned share, and a continuing flow. The channel keeps payment distinct from botanical yield while preserving their common productive-return invariant.

### 9. P2 (35:15-30) Receptive Thresholds
- Semantic invariant: Perception depends on whether light, sound, and life can cross a receptive threshold.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:19-22 contrast blind and seeing, shadow and heat, living and dead, and hearing with the silence of graves.
- Surprising reach: The contrasts become mechanisms of access: inward insight, blinding noon, induced hearing, and the grave as a sealed or sunken enclosure.

#### Subchannel A. Eye, blindness, and inward sight
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The eye receives a visible object, but full perception requires an inward faculty that can be present or absent independently of eyesight.
- Active motifs: eyesight (quranic:root_000121:B001/m01); insight of the heart (quranic:root_000121:B002/m01); loss of eyesight (quranic:root_001049:B001/m01); blindness of heart and judgment (quranic:root_001049:B002/m01); sight by eye or insight (quranic:root_000531:B001/m01); disclosure to the knower (quranic:root_001040:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:19 `بَصِيرُ/أَعْمَىٰ` (ب ص ر/ع م ي); 35:27 `تَرَ` (ر ء ي); 35:28 `عُلَمَٰٓؤُا۟` (ع ل م).
- Synthesis: Physical sight and inward discernment form separate but coordinated operations. The contrast at 35:19 therefore reaches beyond ocular capacity to the heart's ability to recognize what vision presents.

#### Subchannel B. Light, shade, and blinding heat
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Illumination exposes, shade interposes a protective cover, and extreme noon heat can itself disable sight.
- Active motifs: illumination (quranic:root_001564:B001/m01); shade as covering (quranic:root_000966:B001/m01); overhead shade or canopy (quranic:root_000966:B003/m01); heat opposed to cold (quranic:root_000306:B001/m01); blinding midday heat (quranic:root_001049:B008/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:19 `أَعْمَىٰ` (ع م ي); 35:20, 35:25 `نُّورُ/مُنِيرِ` (ن و ر); 35:21 `ظِّلُّ/حَرُورُ` (ظ ل ل/ح ر ر).
- Synthesis: Visibility is regulated by exposure and cover, not by light alone. The latent blinding noon image complicates the simple equation of brightness with sight: excessive exposure can close the same perceptual threshold that ordinary light opens.

#### Subchannel C. Hearing across the life-death boundary
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Sound is received and understood by living participants, while the dead are enclosed in a place whose depth and closure block ordinary response.
- Active motifs: auditory reception (quranic:root_000741:B001/m01); understanding and compliance after hearing (quranic:root_000741:B003/m01); causing another to hear (quranic:root_000741:B004/m01); living animate being (quranic:root_000383:B003/m01); loss of life and force (quranic:root_001454:B001/m01); burial enclosure (quranic:root_001195:B001/m01); sunken or sealed recess (quranic:root_001195:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:22 `أَحْيَآءُ/أَمْوَٰتُ/يُسْمِعُ/مُسْمِعٍ/قُبُورِ` (ح ي ي/م و ت/س م ع/ق ب ر).
- Synthesis: Hearing is staged as a trans-boundary operation whose ordinary participants are alive and reachable. The grave's lexical depth and enclosure materialize why a merely human call cannot establish the missing response circuit.

### 10. P2 (35:15-30) Burden and Liability
- Semantic invariant: A burden remains attached to its bearer unless a valid relation of surety, right, or relief changes its legal or physical disposition.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:18 denies transfer of one burden to another bearer and then turns to purification and final return.
- Surprising reach: The moral image expands into transport equipment, guarantee, debt, and legal entitlement, distinguishing physical carriage from juridical responsibility.

#### Subchannel A. Load, weight, and attempted relief
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A heavy load slows its bearer and is carried bodily or as an entrusted obligation, yet it remains attached to that bearer.
- Active motifs: heaviness and slowed movement (quranic:root_000202:B006/m01); externally carried load (quranic:root_000357:B001/m01); entrusted burden or fault (quranic:root_000357:B003/m01); heavy moral load (quranic:root_001643:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:18 `مُثْقَلَةٌ/حِمْلِ/يُحْمَلْ/تَزِرُ/وَازِرَةٌ/وِزْرَ` (ث ق ل/ح م ل/و ز ر).
- Synthesis: The verse's burden becomes both weight and entrusted responsibility. Physical carriage and moral liability converge without implying that either can be detached from the accountable bearer.

#### Subchannel B. Surety, attached right, and non-transfer
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A claim follows its owner, a guarantor may stand for another, and a surety can carry a liability without erasing the original right.
- Active motifs: liability or right that follows its bearer (quranic:root_000186:B004/m01); proprietary right (quranic:root_000347:B003/m01); guarantee for damages or debt (quranic:root_000357:B004/m01); surety and standing for another (quranic:root_001332:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:18 `حِمْلِ/يُحْمَلْ/كَانَ` (ح م ل/ك و ن); 35:24 `حَقِّ` (ح ق ق); 35:29 `يَتْلُ` (ت ل و).
- Synthesis: Responsibility is mapped as an attached legal relation rather than a detachable object. Guarantee can add a responsible party, but the following right and original claim remain structurally intact.

#### Subchannel C. Fault, seizure, and protective forgiveness
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An agent is seized for an act and held to account, or is protected from the act's consequence by a covering operation.
- Active motifs: being held to account for fault (quranic:root_000018:B002/m01); forgiveness shielding from consequence (quranic:root_001096:B002/m01); erasure of fault by covering it (quranic:root_001307:B009/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:26 `أَخَذْ/كَفَرُ` (ء خ ذ/ك ف ر); 35:28, 35:30 `غَفُورٌ` (غ ف ر).
- Synthesis: Accountability and forgiveness are opposite operations on the same consequence relation: one enforces the act's effect, while the other interposes protection or effaces the recorded fault.

### 11. P2 (35:15-30) Textual Witness
- Semantic invariant: Truth is stabilized, transmitted, and tested through durable marks, ordered following, and public evidence.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:24-25 bring messengers, clear proofs, scrolls, and an illuminating book, while 35:29 presents following or reciting the book.
- Surprising reach: Textuality includes editing, enrollment, and the bodily or procedural act of following, not only a finished book.

#### Subchannel A. Written artifact, correction, and decree
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Letters are composed into a document, corrected into regular form, and used to fix a judgment or enroll a name.
- Active motifs: writing and written book (quranic:root_000621:B003/m01); ordered letters and written artifact (quranic:root_001283:B002/m01); binding written decree (quranic:root_001283:B003/m01); enrollment in a register (quranic:root_001283:B004/m01); correction of book and account (quranic:root_000306:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:21 `حَرُورُ` (ح ر ر); 35:25 `زُّبُرِ/كِتَٰبِ` (ز ب ر/ك ت ب); 35:29 `كِتَٰبَ` (ك ت ب).
- Synthesis: The text is a worked and regulated artifact. Composition, correction, decree, and enrollment are separate operations that together explain how a writing can function as stable witness.

#### Subchannel B. Following, dispatch, and delivery
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A predecessor is followed, a messenger is dispatched with carried speech, and the message is delivered to its destination.
- Active motifs: following in sequence (quranic:root_000186:B001/m01); sending or dispatch (quranic:root_000563:B001/m01); messenger and carried message (quranic:root_000563:B002/m01); descent or delivery of a thing (quranic:root_001492:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:24-25 `أَرْسَلْ/رُسُلُ` (ر س ل); 35:27 `أَنزَلَ` (ن ز ل); 35:29 `يَتْلُ` (ت ل و).
- Synthesis: The lexical following in 35:29 materializes recitation as ordered attachment to a prior text. Dispatch and delivery extend that order into a communication route with sender, carrier, and recipient.

#### Subchannel C. Truth disclosed against fabrication
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A claim corresponds to reality, is made clear by sign or speech, and confronts both fabricated discourse and the attribution of lying.
- Active motifs: truth corresponding to reality (quranic:root_000347:B001/m01); proof that establishes truth (quranic:root_000347:B005/m01); manifest evidence (quranic:root_000170:B004/m01); explanatory disclosure by speech or sign (quranic:root_000170:B005/m01); falsehood opposed to truth (quranic:root_001290:B001/m01); attribution of lying (quranic:root_001290:B002/m01); fabricated speech (quranic:root_000434:B007/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:16 `خَلْقٍ` (خ ل ق); 35:24 `حَقِّ` (ح ق ق); 35:25 `بَيِّنَٰتِ/يُكَذِّبُ/كَذَّبَ` (ب ي ن/ك ذ ب).
- Synthesis: Evidence is not merely present; it performs disclosure and tests correspondence. Fabrication produces speech without a real referent, while clear proof reconnects the claim to what is fixed.

### 12. P2 (35:15-30) Sufficiency and Exchange
- Semantic invariant: Need is answered through capacity, circulating trade, expenditure, and a return that is complete rather than depleted.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:15 contrasts poverty with sufficiency, and 35:29-30 describes spending, imperishable trade, full wages, and increase.
- Surprising reach: Spiritual dependence is rendered as appetite, market liquidity, bid competition, payroll, and settlement of an account.

#### Subchannel A. Need, appetite, and sufficiency
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Material lack or an unbounded appetite creates dependence, while sufficient means allow an agent to stand without that need.
- Active motifs: material need and poverty (quranic:root_001169:B002/m01); appetite-driven poverty of the self (quranic:root_001169:B004/m01); wealth and independence (quranic:root_001110:B001/m01); sufficiency that stands in for another resource (quranic:root_001110:B002/m01); fortune or worldly means (quranic:root_000227:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:15 `فُقَرَآءُ/غَنِىُّ` (ف ق ر/غ ن ي); 35:16, 35:27 `جَدِيدٍ/جُدَدٌ` (ج د د).
- Synthesis: Poverty is split into external lack and internal appetite, while wealth is split into possession and functional sufficiency. The contrast clarifies that abundance can fail to remove dependence when desire itself remains unbounded.

#### Subchannel B. Trade, liquidity, and market failure
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A skilled trader seeks profit, buyers make goods current, bidders seek increase, and failed circulation ends in stagnation.
- Active motifs: trade for profit (quranic:root_000176:B001/m01); skill in earning (quranic:root_000176:B004/m01); market stagnation leading to spoilage (quranic:root_000164:B002/m01); market circulation and depletion (quranic:root_001537:B001/m01); a market becoming current (quranic:root_001273:B018/m01); competitive bidding for increase (quranic:root_000657:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:18, 35:29 `أَقَامُ` (ق و م); 35:29 `تِجَٰرَةً/تَبُورَ/أَنفَقُ` (ت ج ر/ب و ر/ن ف ق); 35:30 `يَزِيدَ` (ز ي د).
- Synthesis: The imperishable trade is defined by sustained circulation, not by static possession. The same network also displays its failure mode: goods cease to move, value collapses, and the market tends toward spoilage.

#### Subchannel C. Expenditure, full payment, and increase
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Resources leave the giver, a due return is paid without deficiency, and an additional grant exceeds the settled amount.
- Active motifs: outgoing expenditure (quranic:root_001537:B002/m01); complete payment without deficiency (quranic:root_001669:B001/m01); wage for work (quranic:root_000015:B001/m01); increase or growth (quranic:root_000657:B001/m01); surplus beyond need or division (quranic:root_001163:B001/m01); beneficent extra gift (quranic:root_001163:B003/m01); apportioned provision (quranic:root_000560:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:29 `أَنفَقُ/رَزَقْ` (ن ف ق/ر ز ق); 35:30 `يُوَفِّيَ/أُجُورَ/يَزِيدَ/فَضْلِ` (و ف ي/ء ج ر/ز ي د/ف ض ل).
- Synthesis: Payment and grace are sequential rather than synonymous: first the due is made whole, then surplus is added. Expenditure is therefore not simple loss but the outgoing stage of a completed and enlarged return cycle.

### 13. P2 (35:15-30) Chromatic Materialization
- Semantic invariant: Color is a surface state that can differentiate matter, disguise its substrate, or register bodily disorder.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:27-28 repeatedly names differing colors in fruit, mountain bands, people, animals, and livestock.
- Surprising reach: Color extends from classification into metallurgy and pathology, where a coating can counterfeit value and redness or whiteness can mark swelling and disease.

#### Subchannel A. White, red, black, and composite complexion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Distinct visible hues are distributed across objects and combined into each entity's outward complexion.
- Active motifs: whiteness (quranic:root_000168:B001/m01); redness (quranic:root_000356:B001/m01); blackness opposed to white (quranic:root_000757:B001/m01); composite visible complexion (quranic:root_001387:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:27 `بِيضٌ/حُمْرٌ/سُودٌ/أَلْوَٰنُ` (ب ي ض/ح م ر/س و د/ل و ن); 35:28 `أَلْوَٰنُ` (ل و ن).
- Synthesis: The motifs form a compact chromatic system: opposed base hues and the composite complexion through which an entity is seen. The system can classify fruit, stone, and living bodies without treating them as one material.

#### Subchannel B. Precious surface and deceptive coating
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A base object is coated with precious metal and thereby made to present the visual value of a different substrate.
- Active motifs: gold as metal (quranic:root_000522:B001/m01); gilding or gold wash (quranic:root_000522:B002/m01); coating base metal with gold or silver (quranic:root_001458:B005/m01); precious gold or silver vessel (quranic:root_001077:B018/m01); patterned or dyed cloth that misrepresents its condition (quranic:root_001290:B009/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:16 `يُذْهِبْ` (ذ ه ب); 35:25 `يُكَذِّبُ/كَذَّبَ` (ك ذ ب); 35:27 `مَآءً/غَرَابِيبُ` (م و ه/غ ر ب).
- Synthesis: Color can be manufactured as a persuasive surface. Gilding and patterned cloth expose the ambiguity between genuine material and a coating that borrows another material's appearance.

#### Subchannel C. Color as swelling and disease
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A bodily or animal disorder emerges as a visible swelling whose white or red coloration marks pathology.
- Active motifs: white swelling in a horse or limb (quranic:root_000168:B012/m01); reddened swelling (quranic:root_000356:B006/m01); animal disease from surfeit (quranic:root_000356:B007/m01); ulcer or swelling emerging from the body (quranic:root_000400:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:27 `بِيضٌ/حُمْرٌ/أَخْرَجْ` (ب ي ض/ح م ر/خ ر ج).
- Synthesis: The same hue terms that pattern mountains can diagnose a body. Color becomes an index of an underlying process, with emergence and swelling explaining how an interior disorder becomes externally legible.

### 14. P2 (35:15-30) Mountain-Water Construction
- Semantic invariant: Hard terrain is made useful by finding, cutting, lining, and retaining water-bearing cavities.
- Surface relation: indirect; 35:27 presents mountains, bands, and descending water, while 35:15-18 supplies roots whose latent senses concern holes, hardness, and load.
- Surprising reach: The mountain image resolves into a practical excavation scene with resistant rock, a lined well, a hidden depression, and emerging water.

#### Subchannel A. Hard face and resistant excavation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An excavator encounters a raised hard mass, tests its surface, and must work against compact material that stops digging.
- Active motifs: raised hard mass (quranic:root_000217:B001/m01); hardness that stops excavation (quranic:root_000217:B005/m01); flat hard ground-face (quranic:root_000227:B007/m01); compact earth and cohesion (quranic:root_001008:B007/m01); broad smooth ground (quranic:root_000766:B009/m01); smoothed and leveled surface (quranic:root_000434:B008/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:16 `خَلْقٍ/جَدِيدٍ` (خ ل ق/ج د د); 35:17, 35:28 `عَزِيزٍ/عَزِيزٌ` (ع ز ز); 35:19, 35:22 `يَسْتَوِى` (س و ي); 35:27 `جِبَالِ/جُدَدٌ` (ج ب ل/ج د د).
- Synthesis: Mountain hardness is not a generic solidity motif; it is resistance encountered by a digging operation. Flatness, cohesion, and the stoppage of excavation define the worked face of the terrain.

#### Subchannel B. Lined well and retained water
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A depression is identified, excavated or lined, and made stable enough to collect and release water.
- Active motifs: well in pasture ground (quranic:root_000227:B010/m01); stone lining and strengthening of a well (quranic:root_000621:B001/m01); rock cavity retaining rainwater (quranic:root_000434:B011/m01); water emerging and increasing (quranic:root_001458:B002/m01); hole or water outlet in ground (quranic:root_001169:B005/m01); hidden depression (quranic:root_001117:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:15 `فُقَرَآءُ` (ف ق ر); 35:16 `خَلْقٍ/جَدِيدٍ` (خ ل ق/ج د د); 35:18 `غَيْبِ` (غ ي ب); 35:25 `زُّبُرِ` (ز ب ر); 35:27 `مَآءً/جُدَدٌ` (م و ه/ج د د).
- Synthesis: Water usefulness depends on constructed containment. The hidden or natural depression becomes a reliable well only when its walls and substrate are made firm enough to hold what emerges.

### 15. P2 (35:15-30) Hidden-Directed Devotion
- Semantic invariant: A warning reaches a receptive person who fears what is unseen and answers through disciplined worship and self-purification.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:18 joins warning, fear of the unseen, prayer, and purification, while 35:28-29 returns to fear, service, and prayer.
- Surprising reach: Fear is not passive affect: it is an alerting signal that changes attention, conduct, and ritual orientation.

#### Subchannel A. Warning that awakens fear of the hidden
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A warning announces danger, fear receives it with gravity, and attention is oriented toward what remains concealed from sight.
- Active motifs: warning that awakens caution (quranic:root_001488:B001/m01); fear joined with awe (quranic:root_000413:B001/m01); concealment from ordinary sight (quranic:root_001117:B001/m01); understanding and compliance after hearing (quranic:root_000741:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:18 `تُنذِرُ/يَخْشَ/غَيْبِ` (ن ذ ر/خ ش ي/غ ي ب); 35:22 `يُسْمِعُ/مُسْمِعٍ` (س م ع); 35:23-24 `نَذِيرٌ/نَذِيرًا` (ن ذ ر); 35:28 `يَخْشَى` (خ ش ي).
- Synthesis: Warning functions as an attention-changing communication, not merely a threat. Its successful reception is shown by hearing that becomes compliance and by fear directed toward a reality not available to ordinary sight.

#### Subchannel B. Worship, prayer, and purification
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A servant submits in a specific ritual act and removes impurity from the self.
- Active motifs: prescribed worship or prayer (quranic:root_000879:B003/m01); worshipful obedience (quranic:root_000973:B003/m01); purification and moral fitness (quranic:root_000637:B002/m01); deity as object of worship (quranic:root_000047:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:15 `ٱللَّهِ/ٱللَّهُ` (ء ل ه); 35:18 `صَّلَوٰةَ/تَزَكَّىٰ/يَتَزَكَّىٰ` (ص ل و/ز ك و); 35:28 `عِبَادِ` (ع ب د); 35:29 `صَّلَوٰةَ` (ص ل و).
- Synthesis: The response to warning is embodied as ritual and purification. Worship names the relation of obedience, prayer supplies its specific practice, and purification names the change produced in the practitioner.

### 16. P2 (35:15-30) Descent, Yield, and Renewal
- Semantic invariant: Delivered water activates a productive cycle in which plants emerge, fruit matures, and lost growth returns.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:27 descends water and brings out fruits, while 35:30 names increase.
- Surprising reach: The cycle includes irrigation technique, recurrent vegetation after loss, flower emergence, and fruit as both produce and beneficial outcome.

#### Subchannel A. Water delivered from above
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Water descends, is conveyed by pouring or irrigation, and becomes a life-bearing resource.
- Active motifs: downward arrival (quranic:root_001492:B001/m01); delivery or bringing down (quranic:root_001492:B002/m01); water as substance (quranic:root_001458:B001/m01); irrigation by pouring water (quranic:root_001458:B003/m01); sky or overhead source (quranic:root_000745:B004/m01); rain as provision (quranic:root_000560:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:27 `أَنزَلَ/مَآءً/سَّمَآءِ` (ن ز ل/م و ه/س م و); 35:29 `رَزَقْ` (ر ز ق).
- Synthesis: Descent is separated from delivery and from the substance delivered. This gives the rain scene a precise source-route-resource structure that can support the subsequent emergence of fruit.

#### Subchannel B. Fruit, returning growth, and increase
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Plants flower and bear mature fruit; after prior loss or dryness, replacement growth returns and increases.
- Active motifs: mature fruit (quranic:root_000205:B001/m01); beneficial result as fruit (quranic:root_000205:B003/m01); replacement vegetation after loss (quranic:root_000433:B010/m01); tender shoot emergence (quranic:root_000810:B004/m01); blossom of a tree (quranic:root_001564:B004/m01); increase and growth (quranic:root_000657:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:20, 35:25 `نُّورُ/مُنِيرِ` (ن و ر); 35:27 `ثَمَرَٰتٍ/مُّخْتَلِفًا` (ث م ر/خ ل ف); 35:28 `مُخْتَلِفٌ` (خ ل ف); 35:30 `شَكُورٌ/يَزِيدَ` (ش ك ر/ز ي د).
- Synthesis: Yield is both a botanical product and a generated benefit, but those senses remain distinct within the same productive sequence. Returning growth makes increase a renewal after loss rather than mere accumulation.

### 17. P3 (35:31-45) Textual Inheritance and Selection
- Semantic invariant: A truthful communication is fixed as a transmissible text, handed on as an inheritance, and entrusted to a differentiated body of recipients.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:31 presents revelation as the confirming truth, and 35:32 gives the Book as an inheritance to selected servants.
- Surprising reach: The transfer can be read simultaneously as inscription, gratuitous grant, succession, and a graded movement in which restraint and precedence are distinct responses to the same trust.

#### Subchannel A. Hidden communication made durable
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Knowledge is delivered through a hidden communicative act, fixed in writing, and authenticated by correspondence with an already established truth.
- Active motifs: hidden delivery of knowledge (quranic:root_001633:B001/m01); writing or inscription (quranic:root_001633:B003/m01); divine revelation (quranic:root_001633:B004/m01); written book (quranic:root_001283:B002/m01); truth as stable correspondence (quranic:root_000347:B001/m01); truthfulness in report (quranic:root_000852:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:31 `أَوْحَيْ/كِتَٰبِ/حَقُّ/مُصَدِّقًا` (و ح ي/ك ت ب/ح ق ق/ص د ق).
- Synthesis: Revelation is not left as an inward event. Its hidden delivery becomes publicly durable as writing, while confirmation tests that writing against a truth relation rather than against novelty alone.

#### Subchannel B. Inheritance, register, and succession
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A predecessor's possession or knowledge passes to successors, is entered into a durable register, and becomes a trust held in the place of those before them.
- Active motifs: inheritance from a predecessor (quranic:root_001639:B001/m01); grant without purchase or contract (quranic:root_001639:B002/m01); inheritance of knowledge or scripture (quranic:root_001639:B003/m01); written register (quranic:root_001283:B004/m01); succession or replacement (quranic:root_000433:B001/m01); what is already present or preceding (quranic:root_001693:B008/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:31 `كِتَٰبِ/يَدَيْ` (ك ت ب/ي د ي); 35:32 `أَوْرَثْ/كِتَٰبَ` (و ر ث/ك ت ب); 35:39 `خَلَٰٓئِفَ` (خ ل ف).
- Synthesis: The Book is both property passed on and knowledge placed in custody. The register supplies durability, succession supplies office, and the gratuitous grant excludes acquisition as the basis of authority.

#### Subchannel C. Selection and graded movement
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A body is purified and selected, then differentiates into one who falls short, one who keeps a middle course, and one who moves ahead.
- Active motifs: purification from admixture (quranic:root_000873:B001/m01); choosing an elite (quranic:root_000873:B002/m01); preference and selection (quranic:root_000452:B002/m01); deliberate choosing of the better (quranic:root_000452:B003/m01); precedence in a race (quranic:root_000671:B001/m01); moderation between extremes (quranic:root_001230:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:32 `ٱصْطَفَيْ/خَيْرَٰتِ/سَابِقٌۢ/مُّقْتَصِدٌ` (ص ف و/خ ي ر/س ب ق/ق ص د).
- Synthesis: Selection establishes a common entrusted body but does not erase internal difference. The race and middle-course motifs turn the three classes into distinct modes of movement under one prior act of choosing.

### 18. P3 (35:31-45) Settled Dwelling and Adornment
- Semantic invariant: Arrival becomes secure habitation when entry, residence, bodily adornment, and relief from exhausting contact all stabilize together.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:33-35 moves from entry into gardens through jewelry and clothing to a lasting dwelling untouched by weariness.
- Surprising reach: The scene is architectural and somatic at once: a granted residence is fitted to the body with wrist ornaments and garments, while pain, grief, and fatigue lose the capacity to make contact.

#### Subchannel A. Entry and lasting residence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Entrants cross a threshold, are settled in a house-like station, and remain there as residents rather than temporary visitors.
- Active motifs: permanent residence (quranic:root_000992:B001/m01); settling in a place (quranic:root_000351:B002/m01); dwelling or fixed abode (quranic:root_001273:B006/m01); resident attached to a house (quranic:root_000499:B007/m01); entering an enclosure (quranic:root_000464:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:33 `يَدْخُلُ` (د خ ل); 35:35 `أَحَلَّ/دَارَ/مُقَامَةِ` (ح ل ل/د و ر/ق و م). The surface root ع د ن is unavailable in the bundle, so its permanent-residence motif cannot receive a token-level anchor.
- Synthesis: Entry is only the threshold operation. Settlement, house attachment, and permanent residence convert passage into a stable condition whose defining feature is not merely location but non-expulsion.

#### Subchannel B. Ornament fitted to the body
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Precious material is fashioned into wrist ornaments and paired with lustrous stones and clothing that complete the inhabitant's visible presentation.
- Active motifs: bodily ornament (quranic:root_000353:B001/m01); bracelet worn at the wrist (quranic:root_000758:B004/m01); wrist ornament (quranic:root_001424:B006/m01); gold as precious material (quranic:root_000522:B001/m01); pearl as ornament (quranic:root_001337:B001/m01); pearl-like lustre (quranic:root_001337:B002/m01); garment worn on the body (quranic:root_001341:B001/m01); silk fabric (quranic:root_000306:B010/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:33 `يُحَلَّ/أَسَاوِرَ/ذَهَبٍ/لُؤْلُؤًا/لِبَاسُ/حَرِيرٌ` (ح ل ي/س و ر/ذ ه ب/ل ء ل ء/ل ب س/ح ر ر); 35:41 `يُمْسِكُ/أَمْسَكَ` (م س ك).
- Synthesis: Adornment is assembled as a material sequence: substance, formed ornament, bodily placement, lustre, and garment. This keeps the scene from collapsing into a generic sign of wealth.

#### Subchannel C. Contact without grief or fatigue
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Grief is removed, and neither effort nor exhaustion can reach the settled inhabitants as an affecting contact.
- Active motifs: toil and fatigue (quranic:root_001507:B004/m01); weakness from exhaustion (quranic:root_001360:B001/m01); an effect reaching a person by contact (quranic:root_001423:B004/m01); grief as inward roughness (quranic:root_000317:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 35:34 `حَزَنَ` (ح ز ن); 35:35 `يَمَسُّ/نَصَبٌ/لُغُوبٌ` (م س س/ن ص ب/ل غ ب).
- Synthesis: Relief is expressed as failed transmission: fatigue and weariness no longer touch the body, while grief has already departed the interior. The dwelling is therefore secure at both the external and affective boundaries.

### 19. P3 (35:31-45) Interior Knowledge and Disclosure
- Semantic invariant: What is hidden within or beyond ordinary perception remains fully knowable and can be brought into evidentiary view.
- Surface relation: direct; 35:31 and 35:38 join divine seeing with knowledge of the unseen and of what chests contain, while 35:40 and 35:44 demand visible evidence and reflective looking.
- Surprising reach: The channel distinguishes concealment, inward source, visual reception, insight, and public manifestation instead of treating knowledge and sight as interchangeable.

#### Subchannel A. Concealed source and inward content
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A content is withheld from ordinary eyes, lodged in the chest or heart as a source of action, yet remains available to complete knowledge.
